# Skills · F · part 63

996 entries, fixed-income to fkit-inspect. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **fixed-income**: Bond analysis methodology covering yield curves, duration, credit spreads, and fixed income portfolio construction ([FoundationalResearch/skills](https://github.com/FoundationalResearch/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fixed-income))
- **fixed-income-analyst**: Use when analyzing bonds, yield curves, duration/convexity, credit spreads, fixed income ETFs, sovereign debt, TIPS breakevens, bond futures, OIS curves, carry/roll-down strategies, or repo markets. Handles rate risk quantification, curve trade construction, credit analysis, inflation-linked products, and central bank policy path pricing. Do NOT use for equity valuation (route to fundamental-analyst), FX forward pricing (route to forex-trader), mortgage underwriting (route to accountant), or corporate credit ratings (route to financial-security). ([zeroes-ones/Skills](https://github.com/zeroes-ones/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/14-finance/fixed-income-analyst) · ★4)
- **fixed-income-attribution**: Fixed income performance attribution: decompose portfolio returns into duration, credit, curve, spread, and currency effects. Campisi or Brinson framework with full source-of-return breakdown. ([arjundrath-star/klade-analyst](https://github.com/arjundrath-star/klade-analyst/tree/HEAD/agent-template/skills/fixed-income-attribution) · 5 copies)
- **fixed-income-context**: Rates and credit view via ETF proxies (SHV, SHY, IEF, TLT, TIP, LQD, HYG, AGG). Reports returns across 1/5/20/60/120 day windows, price percentile vs trailing year, HYG-LQD credit spread delta and TLT-IEF duration spread delta, plus HYG-benchmark correlation. Derives a regime label (risk_off, credit_stress, goldilocks, reflation, rate_pressure, neutral). Every equity valuation implicitly assumes something about rates; this skill closes the equity-only gap without needing FRED. ([rgourley/quant-garage](https://github.com/rgourley/quant-garage/tree/HEAD/skills/fixed-income-context) · ★7)
- **fixed-income-corporate**: Analyze corporate bonds and credit instruments including investment grade and high yield debt. Use when the user asks about corporate bonds, credit spreads (OAS, Z-spread, G-spread), credit ratings, default probabilities, callable bonds, or private credit. Also trigger when users mention 'junk bonds', 'fallen angel', 'yield-to-worst', 'covenant analysis', 'CDS spreads', 'recovery rates', 'direct lending', 'mezzanine debt', 'BBB downgrade risk', or ask how to evaluate corporate credit risk. ([JoelLewis/finance_skills](https://github.com/JoelLewis/finance_skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/wealth-management/skills/fixed-income-corporate) · ★255 · 746 installs · 5 copies)
- **fixed-income-credit-analysis**: Use when analyzing industries or companies for credit decisions in Chinese fixed income markets, building industry analysis frameworks for lending or bond investment, evaluating credit quality via dual-track methodology, constructing investment dashboards from public data, validating frameworks against historical defaults, or assessing cross-industry contagion, portfolio concentration, and systemic risk via the system-intelligence layer. Route vague needs to the credit-analysis-router skill. ([tywinlu1988/Credence-China](https://github.com/tywinlu1988/Credence-China/tree/HEAD/dev/.claude/skills/fixed-income-credit-analysis) · ★5 · 2 copies)

- **fixed-income-daily-brief**: 固定收益研究员日报生成技能。当用户要求生成固收日报、债券市场日报、债市复盘、利率日报、资金面日报时使用，包括"固收日报"、"债市日报"、"债券日报"、"利率市场日报"、"资金面日报"、"生成固收日报"、"今日债市复盘"、"固收日报速评"、"债市每日简报"等表述。本技能自动获取资金面、债券市场、央行操作、信用债、同业存单、地方债、二级资本债、永续债、ABS等核心数据，结合市场情绪与政策动态，生成标准化Markdown格式固定收益日报。即使用户只说"今天债市怎么样"、"生成债券日报"、"固收日报"等简短表述也要触发。 ([aliyun/qwen-dianjin](https://github.com/aliyun/qwen-dianjin/tree/HEAD/DianJin-SKILLS/investment-researcher/fixed-income-daily-brief) · ★645)

- **fixed-income-fund-attribution**: 固收类基金（纯债、"固收+"）业绩归因与绩效分析全流程工具。三大归因方法：财报法（利润表拆解）、净值法（Campisi五因子回归：久期/利率曲线/信用利差/违约/可转债）、持仓法（Campisi四效应模型：收入/国债/利差/择券 + Brinson行业归因）。绩效指标覆盖夏普/卡玛/信息比率/索提诺。支持"固收+"多资产分层归因、基金经理久期管理/信用风险/杠杆/择时能力评估。触发词："固收归因"、"债券基金分析"、"Campisi"、"纯债归因"、"固收+"、"债基分析"、"利率债"、"信用债"、"可转债归因"、"久期管理"、"利差分析"、"债券基金绩效"。 ([sliec/shared-files](https://github.com/sliec/shared-files/tree/HEAD/skills/fixed-income-fund-attribution))

- **fixture-new**: Creates a parity fixture — the frozen scenario plus the contract its output must satisfy. Asks which skill and case, what shape the run must produce, and writes input.md and expect.yml. Ends by proving the new fixture actually fails on an empty directory. A fixture that passes when nothing ran is worse than no fixture: it reports coverage that does not exist. The last step exists to catch exactly that. Use when: "/fixture-new", "add a fixture", "cover this skill with a fixture", extending the parity suite to a skill it does not reach yet.
